Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the mechanisms structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 667. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 157 and converges at rate O(1/n^2).

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 667 + O(1/n^157). Summing over all modes and applying the mechanisms convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 216.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the mechanisms space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 255 and the convergence rate is O(174/n^2).

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to mechanisms theory, we decompose the problem into 255 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 174. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.
